Who's gonna be the load balancer? ^QzhJDlgv

Clients will use either
REST or JSON, so
we cannot expect them to have
python and Pyro installed in their
web browsers => we need an API 
gateway to transform the messages
to Pyro calls. ^ggjiiZcb

gateway handle? ^jNtolnUJ

20.000 requests per second ? ^PJffmDiC

Will the Load Balancer support
20.000 requests per second ? ^yjNXf8ww

Will the Load Balancer support
20.000 requests per second ? ^ntYskIN0

Yes it does -> ^WqIDrf8t

be fault tolerant if there is only one? ^BK7lzyM3
